# Batch survey — `server/scripts/data/`

Generated by a throwaway script (SheetJS 0.20.3), since deleted. Read-only: no importer, schema or taxonomy change came with it.

## Summary

- **Folders:** 31, of which **6 empty** (the brief expected 5).
- **Files:** 120 `.xlsx` (the brief expected 123). No other file types are present.
- **Opened with a data sheet:** 120. **Problems:** 0 (§8).
- **Distinct header shapes:** 5 (§2).
- **Data rows:** 1775 (§3).
- **Intent Labels:** 38 file(s) have a cell with no semicolon (§4).
- **Duplicate Task Titles (exact):** 79 titles repeated — 79 within a niche, 0 across niches (§6).
  All of them are whole-row copies: some files repeat every row of the files before them (Freelancers-Consultants, Small Businesses), and one repeats an earlier file outright (Recruiters & HR Batch 4). Unique titles: 1574.

Throughout, "the data sheet" is the sheet whose first ten rows contain a cell matching `Task Title`. Row counts exclude the header and fully blank rows.
